# Functions that provably never run in NTSC 1.02, with the evidence.
# One per line: NAME<TAB>evidence
SPEC0_MakeStatus	MakeStatus starts as SPEC2_MakeStatus; PADSetSpec runs from gmMain_8015FD24 with 5 and from PADInit only when __PADSpec, the disc's bi2.bin pad spec (0 on NTSC 1.02), is nonzero.
SPEC1_MakeStatus	MakeStatus starts as SPEC2_MakeStatus; PADSetSpec runs from gmMain_8015FD24 with 5 and from PADInit only when __PADSpec, the disc's bi2.bin pad spec (0 on NTSC 1.02), is nonzero.
ftPp_SpecialHiThrow2_Anim	The callback of Popo's state 349 (ftPp_MS_SpecialHiThrow2, the ground pull), which no code enters: the up special changes only to 347, 348 and 350 to 356 (ftpopospecialhi.c; its pull, ftPp_SpecialHi_8012280C, and the pull's ledge change, ftPp_SpecialHi_801227AC, both to the air pull, 354), Nana's to 361, 362 and 365 (ftnanaspecialhi.c), and no other code enters the Ice Climbers' own states.
ftPp_SpecialHiThrow2_IASA	The callback of Popo's state 349, which no code enters (see ftPp_SpecialHiThrow2_Anim).
ftPp_SpecialHiThrow2_Phys	The callback of Popo's state 349, which no code enters (see ftPp_SpecialHiThrow2_Anim).
ftPp_SpecialHiThrow2_Coll	The callback of Popo's state 349, which no code enters (see ftPp_SpecialHiThrow2_Anim).
ftPp_SpecialHi_801227AC	Called only from ftPp_SpecialHiThrow2_Coll, the callback of Popo's state 349, which no code enters (see ftPp_SpecialHiThrow2_Anim).

ftDk_SpecialLwEnd1_Anim	The callback of Donkey Kong's state 386 (ftDk_MS_SpecialLwEnd1), which no code enters: the down special changes only to 383, 384 and 385 (ftdonkeyspeciallw.c: ftDk_SpecialLw_Enter, doAnim, doTransition), the cargo states reach at most his cargo attributes' x4_motion_state plus 17 (368, an aerial throw, ftCo_CargoThrow.c) and the heavy states at most his motion_state plus 9 (350), and no other code names his states.

An enemy item exists only on a stage whose file lists its article (Item_80267978 reads it_804A0F60 and asserts "not found zako model data!" otherwise), and stage 0x25 lists none (m6-classic-dbg/cl-vh-ch's boss match: no enemy article loaded), so no Koopa is ever there. Item_8026A158's other path puts an item in hitlag only as atk_victim of another item, which is set only to fighters (an item's catch hitbox, Kirby's inhale, Yoshi's tongue). Landing while already in hitlag needs the Koopa to move in hitlag: Item_802697D4 skips its velocity, keep_clear's nudge is ground-only, grLib's item wind exists only on Rainbow Cruise, Big Blue and Icicle Mountain, and the floor's motion is ground-only, while Mushroom Kingdom's route (the only stage with the Koopa's article seen: its adventure stage and debug Versus 59) has no moving collision lines (its 324 lines identical over 2,277 ticks of play), so a hovering Koopa in hitlag finds no new floor. Task 24's fourth round (scratch bot) saw the landings in play: hitboxes off, state 1 one frame, then state 2.
